- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:

########## 1. ComBitPosition

- **UUID**: `ac91966e-7132-4477-9e70-579f92cdc35a`

- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE

**容器描述**:

Description of a gateway destination. This container allows defining a gateway destination without the configuration of a complete COM signal. This allows adding / changing gateway relations post build without the configuration of new signals.

######## 1. ComIPduHandleId

- **参数名**: `ComIPduHandleId`

- **描述**: The numerical value used as the ID of this I-PDU.
The ComIPduHandleId is required by the API calls to receive Rx I-PDUs from the PduR.

- **描述**: Defines for I-PDUs with ComIPduType TP: If the underlying TP-module supports RX and TX cancellation of ongoing requests.

######## 1. ComIPduDirection

- **UUID**: `bac41692-0f3d-43be-bc85-d3a5d8e8e0bf`
- **参数名**: `ComIPduDirection`

- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `RECEIVE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `SEND` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The direction defines if this I-PDU and the contributing signals and signal groups are sent or received.

######## 2. ComIPduSignalProcessing

- **UUID**: `004f8b3c-7286-4f49-bbec-a7f07e642a5f`
- **参数名**: `ComIPduSignalProcessing`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `DEFERRED`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DEFERRED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `IMMEDIATE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This option determines the call context and the point of time when the signal notification function is called.
- IMMEDIATE: The notification function is called within Com_TxConfirmation() or Com_RxIndication(). Depending on the lower layer interface, this might be in interrupt context.
- DEFERRED: The notification function is called on task level during the next call cycle of Com_MainFunctionRx() or Com_MainFunctionTx().
                      Please note that the signal data of a received I-PDU is not updated until the next call to Com_MainfunctionRx().

######## 3. ComIPduType

- **UUID**: `7e582b5e-774e-40b0-9c2f-d21e3ad22220`
- **参数名**: `ComIPduType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NORMAL`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NORMAL` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `TP` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the lower layer type that is used to transmit/received the I-PDU.
Defines if this I-PDU is a normal I-PDU that can be sent unfragmented or if this is a large I-PDU that shall be sent via the Transport Protocol of the underlying bus.

NORMAL: indicates that the I-PDU is sent/received unfragmented.
TP: indicates that the I-PDU is send/received fragmented by a Transport Protocol.

####### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

######## 1. ComIPduCallout

- **UUID**: `87c9dd62-f177-480a-b52b-31f39c158848`
- **参数名**: `ComIPduCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the existence and the name of the I-PDUs callout function.
For Rx I-PDUs the callout function is called in the context of Com_RxIndication(). For Tx I-PDUs the callout function is called before the transmission is triggered by COM.
If the parameter is undefined no callout function will be called.
The API prototype is generated to Com_Cbk.h and has to be implemented by the application.

######## 2. ComIPduTriggerTransmitCallout

- **UUID**: `32529ae9-7cf5-44e1-ab74-af1efa5bb598`
- **参数名**: `ComIPduTriggerTransmitCallout`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the existence and the name of a trigger callout function for the corresponding I-PDU.
The function is called in the context of Com_TriggerTransmit() before the data are copied to the lower layer.
The prototype is generated to Com_Cbk.h and has to be implemented by the application.

Additional transmission related configuration parameters.

######## 参数配置

此容器包含 **5** 个参数：

######### INTEGER 类型参数

########## 1. ComTxIPduUnusedAreasDefault

- **UUID**: `ecc97b45-c226-444e-988e-606e7bbe1516`
- **参数名**: `ComTxIPduUnusedAreasDefault`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Byte pattern that is used to fill in unused bits within an I-PDU.
The byte-pattern will be repeated throughout the I-PDU before any initial values or update-bits are applied.

######### FLOAT 类型参数

########## 1. ComMinimumDelayTime

- **UUID**: `01ba83ae-3403-4056-8277-75defc352314`
- **参数名**: `ComMinimumDelayTime`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the Minimum Delay Time (MDT) between successive transmissions of this I-PDU.
The MDT is independent of the possible different transmission modes. There is only one minimum delay time parameter for one I-PDU. The minimum delay timer is not reset by changing the transmission mode. Hence, it is not allowed to violate the minimum delay time by transmission mode changes.
It is not possible to monitor the minimum delay time for I-PDUs that are requested using the Com_TriggerTransmit API.

Note: The actual Minimum Delay Time is incremented by one ComTxTimeBase to ensure the minimum delay in case of late receiving Tx confirmations.

########## 2. ComGwRoutingTimeout

- **UUID**: `7894a180-ebc8-11e3-ac10-0800200c9a66`
- **参数名**: `ComGwRoutingTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0.05`
- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Maximum Time between two routing events before a timeout occurs.

######### STRING 类型参数

########## 1. ComMetaDataDefault

- **UUID**: `a3cf3038-165e-4de8-a286-7d3939657d8d`
- **参数名**: `ComMetaDataDefault`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The AUTOSAR COM module uses default MetaData when not triggered by Com_TriggerIPDUSendWithMetaData.

the string is interpreted as a decimal representation of the characters separated by blanks,
e.g. "97 98 100" means a string "abd", where the char "a" is in byte 0 (lowest address), "b" is in byte 1, and "d" is in byte 2 (highest address).

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. ComTxIPduClearUpdateBit

- **UUID**: `3ca4b539-7019-4055-bf99-96c1ae39574a`
- **参数名**: `ComTxIPduClearUpdateBit`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `Transmit`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `Confirmation`
  - `Transmit`
  - `TriggerTransmit`
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines when the update bits shall be cleared.
Transmit: clear update bit in PduR_ComTransmit() context
TriggerTransmit: clear update bit in Com_TriggerTransmit() context
Confirmation: The update-bits are cleared when the transmission of the I-PDU was confirmed.

############ 参数配置

此容器包含 **5** 个参数：

############# INTEGER 类型参数

############## 1. ComTxModeNumberOfRepetitions

- **UUID**: `131b8f89-34e5-4b53-ae7e-0538574e33e4`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeNumberOfRepetitions`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines how often the I-PDU shall be transmitted after a single transmission trigger event.
For the transmission mode DIRECT and the event driven part of transmission mode MIXED.

If the parameter is configured to '0' the transmission is requested once by a trigger-event.

############# FLOAT 类型参数

############## 1. ComTxModeRepetitionPeriod

- **UUID**: `79774c91-5bc0-4bc2-bd88-e31b280b10ed`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeRepetitionPeriod`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the repetition period of the repeated transmissions in case ComTxModeNumberOfRepetitions is configured to a value greater 0 and the ComTxModeMode is configured to DIRECT or MIXED.
In case of the mixed transmission mode only the event driven part is affected.

############## 2. ComTxModeTimeOffset

- **UUID**: `8208115d-abbf-4d42-902b-302342ca6ca5`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeTimeOffset`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the period between the start of the I-PDU by Com_IpduGroupControl and the first transmission request to the lower layer if ComTxModeMode is configured to PERIODIC or MIXED.
In case of the mixed transmission mode only the periodic part is affected.

############## 3. ComTxModeTimePeriod

- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE

In case of the mixed transmission mode only the periodic part is affected.

- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DIRECT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `MIXED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `NONE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `PERIODIC` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The transmission mode determines the Tx properties of the IPDU:
- DIRECT: The message is transmitted in the next Com_MainFunctionTx() call after a trigger event occurred. 
- PERIODIC: The IPDU is transmitted with the specified cycle time (ComTxModeTimePeriod). 
- MIXED: Combines the transmission modes of CYCLIC and DIRECT.
- NONE: COM does not transmit the I-PDU. The transmission must be triggered by the lower layer through Com_TriggerTransmit().
All transmission modes, except NONE, maintain the Minimum Delay Time (ComTxIPduMinimumDelayTime) and delay a transmission if required.

######### 2. ComTxMode

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `148bd97c-1b3e-4f34-b2be-796e644ded11`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `ComTxMode`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the configuration parameters of the AUTOSAR COM module's transmission modes.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **5** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. ComTxModeNumberOfRepetitions

- **UUID**: `131b8f89-34e5-4b53-ae7e-0538574e33e4`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeNumberOfRepetitions`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines how often the I-PDU shall be transmitted after a single transmission trigger event.
For the transmission mode DIRECT and the event driven part of transmission mode MIXED.

If the parameter is configured to '0' the transmission is requested once by a trigger-event.

########### FLOAT 类型参数

############ 1. ComTxModeRepetitionPeriod

- **UUID**: `79774c91-5bc0-4bc2-bd88-e31b280b10ed`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeRepetitionPeriod`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the repetition period of the repeated transmissions in case ComTxModeNumberOfRepetitions is configured to a value greater 0 and the ComTxModeMode is configured to DIRECT or MIXED.
In case of the mixed transmission mode only the event driven part is affected.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. ComTxModeMode

- **UUID**: `55f7b5b1-9b0b-4f70-8a5e-6687b83fb467`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeMode`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DIRECT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `MIXED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `NONE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `PERIODIC` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The transmission mode determines the Tx properties of the IPDU:
- DIRECT: The message is transmitted in the next Com_MainFunctionTx() call after a trigger event occurred. 
- PERIODIC: The IPDU is transmitted with the specified cycle time (ComTxModeTimePeriod). 
- MIXED: Combines the transmission modes of CYCLIC and DIRECT.
- NONE: COM does not transmit the I-PDU. The transmission must be triggered by the lower layer through Com_TriggerTransmit().
All transmission modes, except NONE, maintain the Minimum Delay Time (ComTxIPduMinimumDelayTime) and delay a transmission if required.

Additional transmission related configuration parameters.

###### 参数配置

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. ComTxIPduUnusedAreasDefault

- **UUID**: `ecc97b45-c226-444e-988e-606e7bbe1516`
- **参数名**: `ComTxIPduUnusedAreasDefault`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Byte pattern that is used to fill in unused bits within an I-PDU.
The byte-pattern will be repeated throughout the I-PDU before any initial values or update-bits are applied.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. ComMinimumDelayTime

- **UUID**: `01ba83ae-3403-4056-8277-75defc352314`
- **参数名**: `ComMinimumDelayTime`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the Minimum Delay Time (MDT) between successive transmissions of this I-PDU.
The MDT is independent of the possible different transmission modes. There is only one minimum delay time parameter for one I-PDU. The minimum delay timer is not reset by changing the transmission mode. Hence, it is not allowed to violate the minimum delay time by transmission mode changes.
It is not possible to monitor the minimum delay time for I-PDUs that are requested using the Com_TriggerTransmit API.

Note: The actual Minimum Delay Time is incremented by one ComTxTimeBase to ensure the minimum delay in case of late receiving Tx confirmations.

######## 2. ComGwRoutingTimeout

- **UUID**: `7894a180-ebc8-11e3-ac10-0800200c9a66`
- **参数名**: `ComGwRoutingTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0.05`
- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Maximum Time between two routing events before a timeout occurs.

####### STRING 类型参数

######## 1. ComMetaDataDefault

- **UUID**: `a3cf3038-165e-4de8-a286-7d3939657d8d`
- **参数名**: `ComMetaDataDefault`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: The AUTOSAR COM module uses default MetaData when not triggered by Com_TriggerIPDUSendWithMetaData.

the string is interpreted as a decimal representation of the characters separated by blanks,
e.g. "97 98 100" means a string "abd", where the char "a" is in byte 0 (lowest address), "b" is in byte 1, and "d" is in byte 2 (highest address).

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. ComTxIPduClearUpdateBit

- **UUID**: `3ca4b539-7019-4055-bf99-96c1ae39574a`
- **参数名**: `ComTxIPduClearUpdateBit`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `Transmit`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `Confirmation`
  - `Transmit`
  - `TriggerTransmit`
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines when the update bits shall be cleared.
Transmit: clear update bit in PduR_ComTransmit() context
TriggerTransmit: clear update bit in Com_TriggerTransmit() context
Confirmation: The update-bits are cleared when the transmission of the I-PDU was confirmed.

######### 1. ComTxMode

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `148bd97c-1b3e-4f34-b2be-796e644ded11`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `ComTxMode`
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

This container contains the configuration parameters of the AUTOSAR COM module's transmission modes.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **5** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. ComTxModeNumberOfRepetitions

- **UUID**: `131b8f89-34e5-4b53-ae7e-0538574e33e4`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeNumberOfRepetitions`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines how often the I-PDU shall be transmitted after a single transmission trigger event.
For the transmission mode DIRECT and the event driven part of transmission mode MIXED.

If the parameter is configured to '0' the transmission is requested once by a trigger-event.

########### FLOAT 类型参数

############ 1. ComTxModeRepetitionPeriod

- **UUID**: `79774c91-5bc0-4bc2-bd88-e31b280b10ed`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeRepetitionPeriod`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the repetition period of the repeated transmissions in case ComTxModeNumberOfRepetitions is configured to a value greater 0 and the ComTxModeMode is configured to DIRECT or MIXED.
In case of the mixed transmission mode only the event driven part is affected.

############ 2. ComTxModeTimeOffset

- **UUID**: `8208115d-abbf-4d42-902b-302342ca6ca5`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeTimeOffset`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the period between the start of the I-PDU by Com_IpduGroupControl and the first transmission request to the lower layer if ComTxModeMode is configured to PERIODIC or MIXED.
In case of the mixed transmission mode only the periodic part is affected.

############ 3. ComTxModeTimePeriod

- **UUID**: `01d2bea7-6b06-408d-b5a4-5085b6ffa606`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeTimePeriod`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the cycle time of periodic transmission requests in case ComTxModeMode is configured to PERIODIC or MIXED.
In case of the mixed transmission mode only the periodic part is affected.

########### ENUMERATION 类型参数

############ 1. ComTxModeMode

- **UUID**: `55f7b5b1-9b0b-4f70-8a5e-6687b83fb467`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeMode`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DIRECT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `MIXED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `NONE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `PERIODIC` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The transmission mode determines the Tx properties of the IPDU:
- DIRECT: The message is transmitted in the next Com_MainFunctionTx() call after a trigger event occurred. 
- PERIODIC: The IPDU is transmitted with the specified cycle time (ComTxModeTimePeriod). 
- MIXED: Combines the transmission modes of CYCLIC and DIRECT.
- NONE: COM does not transmit the I-PDU. The transmission must be triggered by the lower layer through Com_TriggerTransmit().
All transmission modes, except NONE, maintain the Minimum Delay Time (ComTxIPduMinimumDelayTime) and delay a transmission if required.

######### INTEGER 类型参数

- **UUID**: `131b8f89-34e5-4b53-ae7e-0538574e33e4`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeNumberOfRepetitions`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..255]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines how often the I-PDU shall be transmitted after a single transmission trigger event.
For the transmission mode DIRECT and the event driven part of transmission mode MIXED.

If the parameter is configured to '0' the transmission is requested once by a trigger-event.

######### FLOAT 类型参数

########## 1. ComTxModeRepetitionPeriod

- **UUID**: `79774c91-5bc0-4bc2-bd88-e31b280b10ed`

- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the repetition period of the repeated transmissions in case ComTxModeNumberOfRepetitions is configured to a value greater 0 and the ComTxModeMode is configured to DIRECT or MIXED.
In case of the mixed transmission mode only the event driven part is affected.

########## 2. ComTxModeTimeOffset

- **UUID**: `8208115d-abbf-4d42-902b-302342ca6ca5`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeTimeOffset`

- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC

- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the period between the start of the I-PDU by Com_IpduGroupControl and the first transmission request to the lower layer if ComTxModeMode is configured to PERIODIC or MIXED.
In case of the mixed transmission mode only the periodic part is affected.

########## 3. ComTxModeTimePeriod

- **UUID**: `01d2bea7-6b06-408d-b5a4-5085b6ffa606`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeTimePeriod`

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the cycle time of periodic transmission requests in case ComTxModeMode is configured to PERIODIC or MIXED.
In case of the mixed transmission mode only the periodic part is affected.

######### ENUMERATION 类型参数

########## 1. ComTxModeMode

- **UUID**: `55f7b5b1-9b0b-4f70-8a5e-6687b83fb467`
- **参数名**: `ComTxModeMode`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `DIRECT` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `MIXED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `NONE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `PERIODIC` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The transmission mode determines the Tx properties of the IPDU:
- DIRECT: The message is transmitted in the next Com_MainFunctionTx() call after a trigger event occurred. 
- PERIODIC: The IPDU is transmitted with the specified cycle time (ComTxModeTimePeriod). 
- MIXED: Combines the transmission modes of CYCLIC and DIRECT.
- NONE: COM does not transmit the I-PDU. The transmission must be triggered by the lower layer through Com_TriggerTransmit().
All transmission modes, except NONE, maintain the Minimum Delay Time (ComTxIPduMinimumDelayTime) and delay a transmission if required.

###### 参数配置

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. ComBitPosition

- **UUID**: `bbe20358-fc67-4e00-a0f8-af06d98080e0`
- **参数名**: `ComBitPosition`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Start position of the signal within the I-PDU. 
The interpretation of the bit position depends on the signal endianness:
BIG_ENDIAN: bit position refers to the MSB of the signal.
LITTLE_ENDIAN: bit position refers to the LSB of the signal.
OPAQUE: bit position refers to bit 0 of the first byte similar to the little endian byte order.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. ComFirstTimeout

- **UUID**: `f91fff91-0de3-4217-931d-5739cf5f93bf`
- **参数名**: `ComFirstTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Timeout time that is used for reception deadline monitoring immediately after start (or restart) of the deadline monitoring service.
If the parameter is undefined or configured to 0, the signal reception is not monitored from the BSW initialization until the I-PDU is received for the first time.
After the first reception ComTimeout will be used o monitor signal reception.

For signals without a configured update-bit, an I-PDU based deadline monitoring is used. The first timeout time is evaluated as smallest first timeout time of all signals and signal groups without configured update-bit of the I-PDU

######## 2. ComTimeout

- **UUID**: `6dc31079-b282-459b-ada3-3c20bc4bfed6`
- **参数名**: `ComTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Timeout time that is used for deadline monitoring.
If the value is configured to '0' or undefined, the signal is not monitored.

For signals without a configured update-bit, an I-PDU based deadline monitoring is used. The timeout time is evaluated as smallest timeout time of all signals and signal groups without configured update-bit of the I-PDU.

####### STRING 类型参数

######## 1. ComSignalDataInvalidValue

- **UUID**: `cf827c84-1376-4989-ae90-d32971812cde`
- **参数名**: `ComSignalDataInvalidValue`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `0`
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the invalid value of the signal. The invalid value is a dedicated value that is transmitted in case the application cannot provide a valid value. The reception of an invalid signal value can trigger special invalid handling on receiver side.

The interpretation of the configured value depends on ComSignalType setting:
- UINT8, UINT16, UINT32, UINT64, SINT8, SINT16, SINT32, SINT64, FLOAT32, FLOAT64
the string is interpreted as integer type ( ..., -2, -1, 0, 1, 2,...).
- BOOLEAN
the string is interpreted as boolean type. Possible values are "0", "1", "true", "false".
- UINT8_N
the string is interpreted as a decimal representation of the characters separated by blanks,
e.g. "97 98 100" means a string "abd", where the char "a" is in byte 0 (lowest address), "b" is in byte 1, and "d" is in byte 2 (highest address).

######## 2. ComSignalInitValue

- **UUID**: `5e454095-d294-4dda-9bf3-4357ec87ee0e`
- **参数名**: `ComSignalInitValue`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:

- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the Initial value of the signal.
The value is set during initialization of the module and if the corresponding I-PDU group is getting started with parameter 'initialize' set to 'true'.

The interpretation of the configured value depends on ComSignalType setting:
- UINT8, UINT16, UINT32, UINT64, SINT8, SINT16, SINT32, SINT64, FLOAT32, FLOAT64
the string is interpreted as integer type ( ..., -2, -1, 0, 1, 2,...).
- BOOLEAN
the string is interpreted as boolean type. Possible values are "0", "1", "true", "false".
- UINT8_N
the string is interpreted as a decimal representation of the characters separated by blanks,
e.g. "97 98 100" means a string "abd", where the char "a" is in byte 0 (lowest address), "b" is in byte 1, and "d" is in byte 2 (highest address).

######## 3. ComRxDataTimeoutSubstitutionValue

- **UUID**: `aa338376-184f-4aba-a784-f1e106d8c4da`
- **参数名**: `ComRxDataTimeoutSubstitutionValue`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik

the signal value will be set to the value of this parameter, if a timeout occurred.
The interpretation of the configured value depends on ComSignalType setting:
- UINT8, UINT16, UINT32, UINT64, SINT8, SINT16, SINT32, SINT64, FLOAT32, FLOAT64
the string is interpreted as integer type ( ..., -2, -1, 0, 1, 2,...).
- BOOLEAN
the string is interpreted as boolean type. Possible values are "0", "1", "true", "false".
- UINT8_N
the string is interpreted as a decimal representation of the characters separated by blanks,
e.g. "97 98 100" means a string "abd", where the char "a" is in byte 0 (lowest address), "b" is in byte 1, and "d" is in byte 2 (highest address).

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. ComDataInvalidAction

- **UUID**: `340cf9c4-5dad-4e43-a189-324c3195cfdc`
- **参数名**: `ComDataInvalidAction`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `NOTIFY`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NOTIFY` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `REPLACE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the action performed upon reception of an invalid signal.

NOTIFY: The configured invalid notification is called and further signal processing (like signal indication) is abandoned. In this case the invalid value is not stored to preserve the last valid received value.
REPLACE: The last received value is replaced by the initial value. Normal signal processing including filtering and handling of notifications does not take place.

######## 2. ComRxDataTimeoutAction

- **UUID**: `b8de52dd-faa3-441e-8f57-0c9a48b98635`
- **参数名**: `ComRxDataTimeoutAction`

  - `REPLACE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If this parameter is configured to 'REPLACE', the initial value is set for this signal if a timeout occurred.
If this parameter is configured to 'NONE' or undefined, the last received value is maintained.

- **参数名**: `ComSignalEndianness`

######## 5. ComTransferProperty

- **UUID**: `c3e0d3ed-bea7-483d-9769-eedbc06eb29a`
- **参数名**: `ComTransferProperty`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `PENDING`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `PENDING` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `TRIGGERED` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE_WITHOUT_REPETITION` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `TRIGGERED_WITHOUT_REPETITION` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The transfer behavior for the transmission mode DIRECT or MIXED when writing to the signal can either be
- TRIGGERED: the transmission of the message is triggered if the signal is written regardless of the signal value
- TRIGGERED_WITHOUT_REPETITION: the transmission of the message is triggered just once without a repetition
- TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE: the transmission of the message is triggered if the signal is written and the signal value has changed
- TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE_WITHOUT_REPETITION: the transmission of the message is triggered just once without a repetition
- PENDING: writing to the signal does not cause direct transmission.

######## 6. ComSignalAccess

- **UUID**: `f16af6e8-f4e5-40e0-863c-e167fa1d5b9c`
- **参数名**: `ComSignalAccess`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ACCESS_UNCLEAR`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ACCESS_UNCLEAR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ACCESS_NEEDED_BY_SWC_OR_COM` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ACCESS_NEEDED_BY_OTHER` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The parameter ComSignalAccess gives the Com generator the information whether a Signal access is needed or not.
All Signals with ComSignalAccess != ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED can be accessed.
All Signals with ComSignalAccess == ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED cannot be accessed.

ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED			The Signal will never be accessed.
ACCESS_UNCLEAR			It is unclear if the Signal will be accessed.
ACCESS_NEEDED_BY_SWC_OR_COM	It is clear that the Signal access is needed, e.g. by COM. This state is set by a validator
ACCESS_NEEDED_BY_OTHER	                The Signal access is declared as to be needed. This state is set by the user.

####### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

######## 1. ComErrorNotification

- **UUID**: `c8579ffc-6cfd-4125-ac7c-a116d84fd02c`
- **参数名**: `ComErrorNotification`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the name and existence of the error notification function that is called in case of transmit errors
The notification is called for not confirmed send requests, if the corresponding I-PDU group is getting stopped or the COM module is de-initialized.

The error notification function is not available for receive signals.

Notification functions that shall not be handled by the RTE must use a function name without the pre-fix 'Rte_'. The prototype will then be generated to the file 'Appl_Cbk.h' and has to be implemented by called module.

######## 2. ComInvalidNotification

- **UUID**: `5a35cce9-95d0-43e4-93c3-aeb6b6825a6f`
- **参数名**: `ComInvalidNotification`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the name and existence of callback function that is called if the signal has been identified to be invalid.
The notification is called if the invalid action is configured to 'NOTIFY' and an invalid value is received.

The call context depends on the configured signal processing of the corresponding I-PDU.

Notification functions that shall not be handled by the RTE must use a function name without the pre-fix 'Rte_'. The prototype will then be generated to the file 'Appl_Cbk.h' and has to be implemented by called module.

######## 3. ComNotification

- **UUID**: `342dd9a3-2381-4c7b-abc2-1c5470dfad10`
- **参数名**: `ComNotification`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the name and existence of a callback function.
The notification is called
for Tx signals, if the transmission of the signal is confirmed,
for Rx signals, if the signal is received.

The call context depends on the configured signal processing of the corresponding I-PDU.

Notification functions that shall not be handled by the RTE must use a function name without the pre-fix 'Rte_'. The prototype will then be generated to the file 'Appl_Cbk.h' and has to be implemented by called module.

######## 4. ComTimeoutNotification

- **UUID**: `d23f1b15-149b-4c6c-8deb-14173fb547bb`

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the name and existence of the callback function that is called, if a timeout for the signal is detected.

Notification functions that shall not be handled by the RTE must use a function name without the pre-fix 'Rte_'. The prototype will then be generated to the file 'Appl_Cbk.h' and has to be implemented by called module.

- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines the filter that is applied on transmit signals. The filter result of each signal is used to determine the TxMode that shall be used for transmitting the I-PDU. 
For receive signal the filter is used to determine whether the reception event is forwarded to the application or not. 

Signal only have a filter if the ComFilter container exists.

##### 19. ComSignalGroup

**容器属性**:

- **容器名称**: `ComSignalGroup`

**容器描述**:

Defines a signal group. Signal groups are used to transmit or receive its included group signals consistently.

- **UUID**: `3f7908af-8430-475e-89fe-33180cb115b8`
- **参数名**: `ComUpdateBitPosition`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]

######## 1. ComSignalGroupArrayAccess

- **UUID**: `ECUC:d0c33bf3-42af-4404-a779-a38b29d790a1`
- **参数名**: `ComSignalGroupArrayAccess`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `false`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines whether the uint8-array based access shall be used for this ComSignalGroup.

####### FLOAT 类型参数

######## 1. ComFirstTimeout

- **UUID**: `94e22711-3922-4b4c-a886-0ac56337a4d9`
- **参数名**: `ComFirstTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Timeout time that is used for reception deadline monitoring immediately after start (or restart) of the deadline monitoring service.
If the parameter is undefined or configured to 0, the signal group reception is not monitored from the BSW initialization until the I-PDU is received for the first time.
After the first reception ComTimeout will be used o monitor signal group reception.

For signal groups without a configured update-bit, an I-PDU based deadline monitoring is used. The first timeout time is evaluated as smallest first timeout time of all signals and signal groups without configured update-bit of the I-PDU.

######## 2. ComTimeout

- **UUID**: `54ffca01-b41f-4eac-88f3-973a9d28487f`
- **参数名**: `ComTimeout`
- **数据类型**: FLOAT
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..3600]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Timeout time that is used for deadline monitoring.
If the value is configured to '0' or undefined, the signal group is not monitored.

For signal groups without a configured update-bit, an I-PDU based deadline monitoring is used. The timeout time is evaluated as smallest timeout time of all signals and signal groups without configured update-bit of the I-PDU.

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. ComDataInvalidAction

- **UUID**: `91a8d4c3-d0b2-4789-a14d-481f726118c9`
- **参数名**: `ComDataInvalidAction`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `NOTIFY`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NOTIFY` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `REPLACE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter defines the action performed if at least one of the included signals is an invalid signal.

NOTIFY: The configured invalid notification is called and further signal processing (like signal indication) is abandoned. In this case the invalid value is not stored to preserve the last valid received value.
REPLACE: The last received values of all group signals are replaced by the initial values. Normal signal processing including filtering and handling of notifications does not take place.

######## 2. ComRxDataTimeoutAction

- **UUID**: `46479409-20d3-49f9-8732-3758116de778`
- **参数名**: `ComRxDataTimeoutAction`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
  - `REPLACE` (来源: AUTOSAR_ECUC)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: If this parameter is configured to 'REPLACE', the initial values of all group signals are set if a timeout occurred.
If this parameter is configured to 'NONE' or undefined, the last received values are maintained.

######## 3. ComTransferProperty

- **UUID**: `169c4362-a9a9-4da8-82b1-b9c331ef936f`
- **参数名**: `ComTransferProperty`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `PENDING`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `PENDING`
  - `TRIGGERED`
  - `TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE`
  - `TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE_WITHOUT_REPETITION`
  - `TRIGGERED_WITHOUT_REPETITION`
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The transfer behavior for the transmission mode DIRECT or MIXED when writing to the signal group can either be
- TRIGGERED: the transmission of the message is triggered if at least one group signal of signal group is written regardless of the group signal value
- TRIGGERED_WITHOUT_REPETITION: the transmission of the message is triggered just once without a repetition
- TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE: the transmission of the message is triggered if at least one group signal of signal group is written and the group signal value has changed
- TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE_WITHOUT_REPETITION: the transmission of the message is triggered just once without a repetition
- PENDING: writing to the signal group does not cause direct transmission.

Note: If at least one group signal of the signal group has a defined transfer property not equal to PENDING, the transfer property of the signal group will be ignored and the trigger will be evaluated per group signal.

######## 4. ComSignalGroupAccess

- **UUID**: `65042931-e755-477a-8e9a-d77da215b628`
- **参数名**: `ComSignalGroupAccess`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ACCESS_UNCLEAR`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ACCESS_UNCLEAR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ACCESS_NEEDED_BY_SWC_OR_COM` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ACCESS_NEEDED_BY_OTHER` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The parameter ComSignalGroupAccess gives the Com generator the information whether a SignalGroup access is needed or not.
All Signals with ComSignalGroupAccess != ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED can be accessed.
All Signals with ComSignalGroupAccess == ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED cannot be accessed.

ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED			The SignalGroup will never be accessed.
ACCESS_UNCLEAR			It is unclear if the SignalGroup will be accessed.
ACCESS_NEEDED_BY_SWC_OR_COM	It is clear that the SignalGroup access is needed, e.g. by COM. This state is set by a validator
ACCESS_NEEDED_BY_OTHER	                The SignalGroup access is declared as to be needed. This state is set by the user.

####### FUNCTION-NAME 类型参数

######## 1. ComErrorNotification

- **UUID**: `163e8d8c-025a-4c5c-b2c3-8b7802ffcfa9`
- **参数名**: `ComErrorNotification`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **函数变体**:
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the name and existence of the error notification function that is called in case of transmit errors
The notification is called for not confirmed send requests, if the corresponding I-PDU group is getting stopped or the COM module is de-initialized.

The error notification function is not available for receive signal groups.

Notification functions that shall not be handled by the RTE must use a function name without the pre-fix 'Rte_'. The prototype will then be generated to the file 'Appl_Cbk.h' and has to be implemented by called module.

######## 2. ComInvalidNotification

- **UUID**: `3664d347-b235-42df-8fe4-590836eb8a2c`
- **参数名**: `ComInvalidNotification`
- **数据类型**: FUNCTION-NAME

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Defines the name and existence of callback function that is called if the signal has been identified to be invalid.
The notification is called if the invalid action is configured to 'NOTIFY' and an invalid value is received.

The call context depends on the configured signal processing of the corresponding I-PDU.

Notification functions that shall not be handled by the RTE must use a function name without the pre-fix 'Rte_'. The prototype will then be generated to the file 'Appl_Cbk.h' and has to be implemented by called module.

########## 1. ComBitPosition

- **UUID**: `6c58fecb-4deb-4471-9ffc-a33fafe33810`
- **参数名**: `ComBitPosition`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Starting position within the I-PDU (not in the shadow buffer).
The interpretation of the bit position depends on the signal endianness:
BIG_ENDIAN: bit position refers to the MSB.
LITTLE_ENDIAN: bit position refers to the LSB.
OPAQUE: bit position refers to bit0 of the first byte like in little endian byte order.

######### STRING 类型参数

########## 1. ComSignalDataInvalidValue

- **UUID**: `ae12d133-53e0-46dd-9a2d-0c07d8a0881c`
- **参数名**: `ComSignalDataInvalidValue`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `0`
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the invalid value of the group signal. The invalid value is a dedicated value that is transmitted in case the application cannot provide a valid value. The reception of an invalid signal value can trigger special invalid handling on receiver side.

The interpretation of the configured value depends on ComSignalType setting:
- UINT8, UINT16, UINT32, UINT64, SINT8, SINT16, SINT32, SINT64, FLOAT32, FLOAT64
the string is interpreted as integer type ( ..., -2, -1, 0, 1, 2,...).
- BOOLEAN
the string is interpreted as boolean type. Possible values are "0", "1", "true", "false".
- UINT8_N
the string is interpreted as a decimal representation of the characters separated by blanks,
e.g. "97 98 100" means a string "abd", where the char "a" is in byte 0 (lowest address), "b" is in byte 1, and "d" is in byte 2 (highest address).

########## 2. ComSignalInitValue

- **UUID**: `682a569b-5a4c-44c9-a843-c25a82321920`
- **参数名**: `ComSignalInitValue`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `0`
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Initial value for this signal.
The value is set during initialization of the module and if the corresponding I-PDU group is getting started with parameter 'initialize' set to 'true'.

Note: The interpretation of the configured string depends on ComSignalType:
- UINT8, UINT16, UINT32, UINT64, SINT8, SINT16, SINT32, SINT64, FLOAT32, FLOAT64
the string is interpreted as integer type ( ..., -2, -1, 0, 1, 2,...).
- BOOLEAN
the string is interpreted as boolean type. It can be one of "0", "1", "true", "false".
- UINT8_N
the string is interpreted as a decimal representation of the characters separated by blanks,
e.g. "97 98 100" means a string "abd", where the char "a" is in byte 0 (lowest address), "b" is in byte 1, and "d" is in byte 2 (highest address).

the string is interpreted as a decimal representation of the characters separated by blanks,

########## 3. ComTransferProperty

- **UUID**: `087d6b46-38c3-4213-b545-d00e90bc629f`
- **参数名**: `ComTransferProperty`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `PENDING`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `PENDING`
  - `TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE`
  - `TRIGGERED`
  - `TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE_WITHOUT_REPETITION`
  - `TRIGGERED_WITHOUT_REPETITION`
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The transfer behavior for the transmission mode DIRECT or MIXED when writing to the group signal can either be
- TRIGGERED: the transmission of the message is triggered if the group signal is written regardless of the group signal value
- TRIGGERED_WITHOUT_REPETITION: the transmission of the message is triggered just once without a repetition
- TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE: the transmission of the message is triggered if the group signal is written and the group signal value has changed
- TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE_WITHOUT_REPETITION: the transmission of the message is triggered just once without a repetition
- PENDING: writing to the group signal does not cause direct transmission.

Note: If all the transfer properties of the group signals of a signal group equals PENDING, the transfer property of the signal group will be evaluated for the group signals.

All GroupSignals with ComSignalAccess != ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED can be accessed.
All GroupSignals with ComSignalAccess == ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED cannot be accessed.

ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED			The GroupSignal will never be accessed.
ACCESS_UNCLEAR			It is unclear if the GroupSignal will be accessed.
ACCESS_NEEDED_BY_SWC_OR_COM	It is clear that the GroupSignal access is needed, e.g. by COM. This state is set by a validator
ACCESS_NEEDED_BY_OTHER	                The GroupSignal access is declared as to be needed. This state is set by the user.

######## 配置容器

本模块包含 **1** 个配置容器：

######### 1. ComFilter

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `7cc5dfdd-ffe9-4554-a6cf-8111ab51fc56`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `ComFilter`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines the filter that is applied on transmit signals. The filter result of each signal is used to determine the TxMode that shall be used for transmitting the I-PDU. 
For receive signal the filter is used to determine whether the reception event is forwarded to the application or not. 

Signal only have a filter if the ComFilter container exists.

########## 参数配置

此容器包含 **7** 个参数：

########### INTEGER 类型参数

############ 1. ComFilterMask

- **UUID**: `af384335-0d6d-4be4-8b19-57aa2cbaf141`
- **参数名**: `ComFilterMask`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `18446744073709551615`
- **取值范围**: [0..18446744073709551615]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Only valid on sender side: The supported filter algorithms for Tx signals are:
- None: The signal has no filter (Multiplicity of ComFilter)
- Always: This filter always evaluates to TRUE
- Never: This filter always evaluates to FALSE
- MaskedNewDiffersMaskedOld: ((new & mask) != (old & mask))
- MaskedNewEqualsX:  ((new & mask) == x)
- MaskedNewDiffersX:  ((new & mask) != x)
- NewIsOutside: ((new < min) || (max < new))
- NewIsWithin: ((min <= new) && (new <= max))

############ 2. ComFilterMax

- **UUID**: `98db3214-c8dc-4a97-bd04-27490643efad`
- **参数名**: `ComFilterMax`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [-2147483648..4294967295]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The specified max value is used for the filters:
- NewIsOutside: ((new < min) || (max < new))
- NewIsWithin: ((min <= new) && (new <= max))

############ 3. ComFilterMin

- **UUID**: `6f57fdd7-3bdc-4b89-a63c-71f5be69eb27`
- **参数名**: `ComFilterMin`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER

############ 4. ComFilterX

- **UUID**: `97beafe7-a382-43be-ae41-38a31e3ca0d3`
- **参数名**: `ComFilterX`

####### 2. ComFilter

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `7cc5dfdd-ffe9-4554-a6cf-8111ab51fc56`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `ComFilter`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines the filter that is applied on transmit signals. The filter result of each signal is used to determine the TxMode that shall be used for transmitting the I-PDU. 
For receive signal the filter is used to determine whether the reception event is forwarded to the application or not. 

Signal only have a filter if the ComFilter container exists.

##### 20. ComGroupSignal

**容器属性**:

**容器描述**:

Specifies a group signal that is included within the signal group.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **11** 个参数：

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. ComBitPosition

- **UUID**: `6c58fecb-4deb-4471-9ffc-a33fafe33810`
- **参数名**: `ComBitPosition`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `0`
- **取值范围**: [0..65535]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Starting position within the I-PDU (not in the shadow buffer).
The interpretation of the bit position depends on the signal endianness:
BIG_ENDIAN: bit position refers to the MSB.
LITTLE_ENDIAN: bit position refers to the LSB.
OPAQUE: bit position refers to bit0 of the first byte like in little endian byte order.

######## 2. ComBitSize

- **UUID**: `bdc18b82-94b3-4870-af1a-afbaee0f2273`

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Size in bits, for non-array signal types.

####### STRING 类型参数

######## 1. ComSignalDataInvalidValue

- **UUID**: `ae12d133-53e0-46dd-9a2d-0c07d8a0881c`
- **参数名**: `ComSignalDataInvalidValue`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `0`
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines the invalid value of the group signal. The invalid value is a dedicated value that is transmitted in case the application cannot provide a valid value. The reception of an invalid signal value can trigger special invalid handling on receiver side.

The interpretation of the configured value depends on ComSignalType setting:
- UINT8, UINT16, UINT32, UINT64, SINT8, SINT16, SINT32, SINT64, FLOAT32, FLOAT64
the string is interpreted as integer type ( ..., -2, -1, 0, 1, 2,...).
- BOOLEAN
the string is interpreted as boolean type. Possible values are "0", "1", "true", "false".
- UINT8_N
the string is interpreted as a decimal representation of the characters separated by blanks,
e.g. "97 98 100" means a string "abd", where the char "a" is in byte 0 (lowest address), "b" is in byte 1, and "d" is in byte 2 (highest address).

######## 2. ComSignalInitValue

- **UUID**: `682a569b-5a4c-44c9-a843-c25a82321920`
- **参数名**: `ComSignalInitValue`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `0`
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Initial value for this signal.
The value is set during initialization of the module and if the corresponding I-PDU group is getting started with parameter 'initialize' set to 'true'.

Note: The interpretation of the configured string depends on ComSignalType:
- UINT8, UINT16, UINT32, UINT64, SINT8, SINT16, SINT32, SINT64, FLOAT32, FLOAT64
the string is interpreted as integer type ( ..., -2, -1, 0, 1, 2,...).
- BOOLEAN
the string is interpreted as boolean type. It can be one of "0", "1", "true", "false".
- UINT8_N
the string is interpreted as a decimal representation of the characters separated by blanks,
e.g. "97 98 100" means a string "abd", where the char "a" is in byte 0 (lowest address), "b" is in byte 1, and "d" is in byte 2 (highest address).

######## 3. ComRxDataTimeoutSubstitutionValue

- **UUID**: `3eff9926-964f-48e4-b94c-caa084946c31`
- **参数名**: `ComRxDataTimeoutSubstitutionValue`
- **数据类型**: STRING
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **字符串变体**:
  - 默认值: `0`
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: If this parameter and the ComRxDataTimeoutAction is configured to 'REPLACE',
the signal value will be set to the value of this parameter, if a timeout occurred.
The interpretation of the configured value depends on ComSignalType setting:
- UINT8, UINT16, UINT32, UINT64, SINT8, SINT16, SINT32, SINT64, FLOAT32, FLOAT64
the string is interpreted as integer type ( ..., -2, -1, 0, 1, 2,...).
- BOOLEAN
the string is interpreted as boolean type. Possible values are "0", "1", "true", "false".
- UINT8_N
the string is interpreted as a decimal representation of the characters separated by blanks,
e.g. "97 98 100" means a string "abd", where the char "a" is in byte 0 (lowest address), "b" is in byte 1, and "d" is in byte 2 (highest address).

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. ComSignalEndianness

- **UUID**: `6838cdd4-d558-4a50-8172-70db42cb1096`
- **参数名**: `ComSignalEndianness`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `LITTLE_ENDIAN`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE

OPAQUE indicates that no byte order conversion is performed (e.g. for byte arrays).

######## 2. ComSignalType

- **UUID**: `92f010bf-776c-44cb-a643-4b28c0699b2b`
- **参数名**: `ComSignalType`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `UINT8`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false

  - `SINT64`

  - `SINT8`
  - `UINT16`
  - `UINT32`
  - `UINT64`
  - `UINT8`
  - `UINT8_DYN`
  - `UINT8_N`
  - `FLOAT32`
  - `FLOAT64`
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE

######## 3. ComTransferProperty

- **UUID**: `087d6b46-38c3-4213-b545-d00e90bc629f`
- **参数名**: `ComTransferProperty`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **默认值**: `PENDING`
- **来源**: AUTOSAR_ECUC
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `PENDING`
  - `TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE`
  - `TRIGGERED`
  - `TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE_WITHOUT_REPETITION`
  - `TRIGGERED_WITHOUT_REPETITION`
- **实现配置类**:
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The transfer behavior for the transmission mode DIRECT or MIXED when writing to the group signal can either be
- TRIGGERED: the transmission of the message is triggered if the group signal is written regardless of the group signal value
- TRIGGERED_WITHOUT_REPETITION: the transmission of the message is triggered just once without a repetition
- TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE: the transmission of the message is triggered if the group signal is written and the group signal value has changed
- TRIGGERED_ON_CHANGE_WITHOUT_REPETITION: the transmission of the message is triggered just once without a repetition
- PENDING: writing to the group signal does not cause direct transmission.

Note: If all the transfer properties of the group signals of a signal group equals PENDING, the transfer property of the signal group will be evaluated for the group signals.

######## 4. ComSignalAccess

- **UUID**: `a7010c92-e79a-4177-afa7-2736ba566e0e`
- **参数名**: `ComSignalAccess`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `ACCESS_UNCLEAR`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ACCESS_UNCLEAR` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ACCESS_NEEDED_BY_SWC_OR_COM` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `ACCESS_NEEDED_BY_OTHER` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: The parameter ComSignalAccess gives the Com generator the information whether a GroupSignal access is needed or not.
All GroupSignals with ComSignalAccess != ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED can be accessed.
All GroupSignals with ComSignalAccess == ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED cannot be accessed.

ACCESS_NOT_NEEDED			The GroupSignal will never be accessed.
ACCESS_UNCLEAR			It is unclear if the GroupSignal will be accessed.
ACCESS_NEEDED_BY_SWC_OR_COM	It is clear that the GroupSignal access is needed, e.g. by COM. This state is set by a validator
ACCESS_NEEDED_BY_OTHER	                The GroupSignal access is declared as to be needed. This state is set by the user.

**容器描述**:

######## 参数配置

##### 21. ComFilter

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `7cc5dfdd-ffe9-4554-a6cf-8111ab51fc56`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `ComFilter`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false
- **多配置容器**: false

**容器描述**:

Defines the filter that is applied on transmit signals. The filter result of each signal is used to determine the TxMode that shall be used for transmitting the I-PDU. 
For receive signal the filter is used to determine whether the reception event is forwarded to the application or not. 

Signal only have a filter if the ComFilter container exists.

##### 25. ComSignalGatewayQueue

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `55440b5c-00c9-4714-b082-f457c9bfbb3d`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `ComSignalGatewayQueue`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

Cross-partition queue is used to perform the routing between different partitions.

Each container consists of two unidirectional cross-partition queues from partition A to partition B and vice versa.
It is possible to specify the queue size for each direction independently.

###### 参数配置

此容器包含 **2** 个参数：

  - POST-BUILD: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
- **描述**: Size of the cross-partition queue for communication from partition A to partition B.

######## 2. ComCrossPartitionQueueSizeBtoA

- **UUID**: `dd91769e-a9d5-428d-ab6a-5127e3b45aef`

##### 26. ComDescriptionRoutingQueue

**容器属性**:

- **UUID**: `8c07748b-ae3e-41b0-8a4b-1ffce3341118`
- **容器类型**: PARAM-CONF-CONTAINER
- **容器名称**: `ComDescriptionRoutingQueue`
- **多重性**: [0..1]
- **构建后可变**: false

**容器描述**:

Cross-partition queue is used to perform the routing between different partitions.

Each container consists of two unidirectional cross-partition queues from partition A to partition B and vice versa.
It is possible to specify the queue size for each direction independently.

- **描述**: Size of the cross-partition queue for communication from partition A to partition B.

######## 9. ComStrictRepetitionPeriod

- **UUID**: `a06cb583-85c2-4263-bb2f-f51d168971a7`
- **参数名**: `ComStrictRepetitionPeriod`
- **数据类型**: BOOLEAN
- **多重性**: [1..1]

- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false

  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Defines how the ComTxModeRepetitionPeriod is affected by the ComMinimumDelayTime.
If the feature is activated, the time between two transmits equals ComTxModeRepetitionPeriod (Autosar 4.0.3).
If the feature is deactivated, the time between two transmits can be shorter than ComTxModeRepetitionPeriod if the first transmit is delayed by the ComMinimumDelayTime (Autosar 4.1.3).

####### ENUMERATION 类型参数

######## 1. ComSignalGateway

- **UUID**: `feb0c203-b9e9-48ec-a44e-77ba07307825`
- **参数名**: `ComSignalGateway`
- **数据类型**: ENUMERATION
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `NONE`
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **可选值**:
  - `NONE` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `MINIMALSIGNALPROCESSING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
  - `COMPLETESIGNALPROCESSING` (来源: Vector Informatik)
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: Define if SignalGateway shall be used and what kind of signal routing shall be used.

NONE: No Signal Gateway shall be used.
MINIMALSIGNALPROCESSING: Endianness conversion, sign extension, check of update bits, I-PDU callout, reset reception deadline monitoring for I-PDU based monitoring is performed, before any signal is routed to its destination (ASR Version 4.3). 
COMPLETESIGNALPROCESSING: Signal invalidation, reception filtering and replace action of signal-based deadline monitoring are performed in addition to the MINIMALSIGNALPROCESSING steps, before any signal is routed to its destination (ASR Version 4.2). 
The resulting value after the complete signal processing is routed to the destination.

####### INTEGER 类型参数

######## 1. ComReduceBoolDataByNegationThreshold

- **UUID**: `deb9f2e1-5506-440e-b4da-1846af77b47e`
- **参数名**: `ComReduceBoolDataByNegationThreshold`
- **数据类型**: INTEGER
- **多重性**: [1..1]
- **默认值**: `2`
- **取值范围**: [0..9223372036854775807]
- **来源**: Vector Informatik
- **符号名值**: false
- **需要索引**: false
- **实现配置类**:
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-PRE-COMPILE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-LOADABLE
  - PRE-COMPILE: VARIANT-POST-BUILD-SELECTABLE
- **描述**: This parameter activates/deactivates the capability to compress boolean CONFIG-CLASS PRE-COMPILE ROM data by using the negation operator.

0: The optimization is deactivated.
>0: If the number of deduplicateable elements is greater than this threshold the data optimization can be performed.

Code: the code size is increased due to the usage of the negation operator in the data access.
RAM: no change expected.
ROM: the ROM size is minimized.
Runtime: the runtime is increased due to the usage of the negation operator in the data access.
